- **worker**: Routine-tier engineer for well-specified edits - carries out a written plan or a mechanical change (a rename, a config rollout, a unit a driver split off, often one of several running in parallel) in its own worktree, proves it with the repo's checks, commits it and stops on any choice the brief does not settle. Use for subagents spawned during a /@PLUGIN@ run, never to run the playbook itself. ([kimnt93/swe-skills](https://github.com/kimnt93/swe-skills/blob/HEAD/src/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Default pstack worker. Mechanical feature and refactor edits, swarm workers, coverage fan-out, explorers that need write access. Runs swe-2-max (free tier) so wide fan-out stays cheap. ([kingdomseed/pstack-devin](https://github.com/kingdomseed/pstack-devin/blob/HEAD/plugins/pstack/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Mechanical sub-task with a complete brief (search, extraction, wide fan-outs, mechanical edits, smoke tests, summaries). Not for judgement work such as reviews, planning or debugging. ([kitfunso/claude-config](https://github.com/kitfunso/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: portolan 规程派出的有界 worker：执行环 fan-out 用，试跑验路径用。其他情形勿用。 ([KKL08/Portolan](https://github.com/KKL08/Portolan/blob/HEAD/portolan-cc/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: A drop-in for a bare general-purpose spawn — Sonnet at medium effort with every tool and no report contract. The supervisor hook routes an unpinned general-purpose spawn here so a cheap worker never inherits the session's effort. Use for research, docs, prose review, or any task that needs no evidence contract; for a spec that needs proof pasted, use supervisor:implementer instead. ([konyklabs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/konyklabs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/supervisor/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements a bounded task and verifies the result ([kott/dotfiles](https://github.com/kott/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/pi/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implementation agent. N instances run in parallel, one task each, in isolated worktrees. Consumes a briefing, verifies touchpoints, implements to locked tests, carries kb updates in the same PR. Stops and reports on any plan/reality conflict. ([kputegnat/firewalker](https://github.com/kputegnat/firewalker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implementation worker for one bounded Clipbox task (a fix, a feature slice, tests, a refactor or a code review) described in a detailed prompt from the coordinator. Works in its own git worktree, commits locally, never pushes. ([Kr1xxxx/COPYBOX_Public](https://github.com/Kr1xxxx/COPYBOX_Public/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Decompile exactly one `.pyc` and repair it until the exact verifier reports `status: "perfect"`. Success is bytecode equivalence, not readable-looking source. ([kser93/pylingual-portable-mcp](https://github.com/kser93/pylingual-portable-mcp/blob/HEAD/src/pylingual_portable/agent_specs/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Cheap parallel worker. General-purpose subagent for mechanical, low-judgment batch tasks that the Architect or coding agents can fan out in parallel — formatting sweeps, bulk renames, file-by-file refactors across many files, lint-rule auto-fixes, and similar small-scope edits. Not for novel design, architecture, or cross-module logic changes. Use many instances in parallel. ([kylerpbyrd/byrdos](https://github.com/kylerpbyrd/byrdos/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Builds an approved plan, or one part of it, from a written brief, on Sonnet. Use it whenever a plan is approved in an Opus session, instead of asking the user to switch models, and for each feature in a /coordinate batch. Write the brief as the coordinate skill describes. Not for changes of one or two files, or work that is mostly judgment. ([LaugeM/stikling](https://github.com/LaugeM/stikling/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Cheap implementation worker for one task file from docs/tasks/ that has a TDD plan and BDD acceptance criteria. Invoke with @worker so the main loop stays on planning. Not for contract, permission, or storage-schema changes. ([laypal/ai-leak-checker](https://github.com/laypal/ai-leak-checker/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Cheap implementation worker for tasks with a fixed spec and an existing test gate (a task file under docs/tasks/ with a TDD plan). ([laypal/ai-leak-checker](https://github.com/laypal/ai-leak-checker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: docs/tasks/의 작업 카드 하나를 받아 구현·검증하고 카드에 결과를 적는다. 오케스트레이터가 카드 경로만 넘겨 호출한다. ([Lcjam/needteacher](https://github.com/Lcjam/needteacher/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))

- **worker**: Implements a single, well-defined work item, usually one step from the planner's plan. Writes code and tests, runs the linter, formatter and tests, and reports what changed. - **worker**: Executes well-specified, low-design tasks: codebase searches, reading long files or logs, repetitive multi-file edits, running commands and tests, verifying claims, fetching docs. - **worker**: 執行已經想清楚的工作。當任務的「做什麼」已經確定、剩下的是動手完成時使用——包含讀寫檔案、跑指令、搜尋整理資料、依既定方向修改內容。不要用它來決定方向、評估取捨、或回答開放式問題；那些留在主對話。 ([Novie131/SmartRouteAI](https://github.com/Novie131/SmartRouteAI/blob/HEAD/plugins/tiered-agents/agents/worker.md))

Runs until backlog is empty or 60-min timeout. ([nterprise-ai/nterprise-dev](https://github.com/nterprise-ai/nterprise-dev/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: [Legacy alias] Use hands faculty instead. See faculties/hands.md. ([nustanakritwithai/Hih](https://github.com/nustanakritwithai/Hih/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md) · 2 copies)
- **worker**: Implements one bounded task packet and runs its focused checks. ([olibartfast/agentic-ai-playground](https://github.com/olibartfast/agentic-ai-playground/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md))
- **worker**: Since 19/10/2026 (spawn speedup, `docs/agent_spawn_latency.md`) the full worker protocol lives in **`D:/Kai/.claude/agents/kai-worker.md`** - the single source of truth. Edit it there, never here. ([omerbengal/Kai](https://github.com/omerbengal/Kai/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Default agent for hands-on implementation — writing code, editing files, running commands/builds/tests, applying fixes. The main session runs on Opus for planning and orchestration; prefer this agent over general-purpose for actual execution work so it runs on Sonnet instead, since Sonnet is fast and cheap enough for well-scoped implementation once the plan is set. Use general-purpose or Explore instead when the task itself requires heavier reasoning (ambiguous investigation, architectural judgment calls) rather than executing a clear plan. ([omznc/loadout](https://github.com/omznc/loadout/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Executes bounded investigation, command-running, coding, and mechanical editing units in an isolated context. Select this agent after delegation is warranted when the unit requires repository inspection, command evidence, scoped edits, or other concrete execution, and should return traceable findings or artifacts for caller integration. ([OopsYao/pi-rill](https://github.com/OopsYao/pi-rill/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Default delegate for well-scoped implementation, research, or review legs, on every session model (Opus included). Model and effort are PINNED (sonnet · effort high) so Agent-tool spawns never inherit the session's model or a deep-work session's xhigh/max effort; a per-call model or effort overrides the pin, so omit both. Use for single scoped tasks; for fan-out with per-stage effort control keep using the Workflow agent() path. ([orcait-co/ywr-harness](https://github.com/orcait-co/ywr-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/ywr-harness/agents/worker.md))

- **worker**: A long-running engineering agent that uses the desk substrate. Do not use it when the agent needs MCP tools, skills, web access or other subagents. ([tonywonguf/claude-work-setup](https://github.com/tonywonguf/claude-work-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-haiku.md))
- **worker-handoff**: From the repository root, with dependencies installed: ([ashlrai/phantom](https://github.com/ashlrai/phantom/blob/HEAD/examples/agents/worker-handoff.md) · ★5)
- **worker-hard**: Implements difficult cross-cutting work involving architecture, migrations, security-sensitive behavior, complex debugging, or substantial uncertainty. Use only when routine Sonnet work is insufficient. ([jonathanmoregard/claude-config](https://github.com/jonathanmoregard/claude-config/blob/HEAD/runtime/agents/worker-hard.md))
- **worker-hard**: 僅在 worker 失敗後由主對話指派的升級版執行者，委派時必須附上前次的失敗報告。不要用於一般任務，也不要跳過 worker 直接派給它。 ([Novie131/Chutora](https://github.com/Novie131/Chutora/blob/HEAD/plugins/chutora/agents/worker-hard.md))
- **worker-heavy**: task-router worker (opus tier). Implements one routed task test-first and ends with a JSON status block. ([omeerdvrn/o-mods](https://github.com/omeerdvrn/o-mods/blob/HEAD/mods/task-router/agents/worker-heavy.md) · ★6)
- **worker-heavy**: heavy-tier worker. Executes exactly one LingoFlux "TASK &lt;id&gt;" brief in its own git worktree and reports in the fixed STATUS format. Choose the variant by task tier - light (mechanical edits, config, docs), standard (feature or fix with a known cause), heavy (cross-layer change, unclear root cause, delicate invariants). ([xuanquangIT/AI-Translate-Extension](https://github.com/xuanquangIT/AI-Translate-Extension/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker-heavy.md) · ★1)
- **worker-heavy**: Use only when the work cannot run at a lower tier and can be split off: architecture and multi-file refactors, contradictions across sources, a bug a lower tier already failed on, deep reasoning on a self-contained problem, anything resting on a figure, a citation or a claim. Largest model. Sweeps and judging against a fixed rubric go to worker-standard. Never for anything under confidential/. ([batutho-dot/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/batutho-dot/ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-heavy.md))
- **worker-heavy**: High-complexity implementation agent for difficult, broad, or high-stakes tasks ([DarkoKuzmanovic/pi-subagents](https://github.com/DarkoKuzmanovic/pi-subagents/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-heavy.md))
- **worker-high**: Sonnet 5.5 at high effort for routine, clearly specified execution that does not need extra-high effort. Use it for reading and summarizing files, searching the repository, simple edits from exact instructions, running a single command or script, and git chores. Use worker for long or many-step work; escalate judgment calls to analyst. ([ahmed0thman/thread](https://github.com/ahmed0thman/thread/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker-high.md) · ★5)
- **worker-high**: General-purpose subagent pinned to high effort, so it does not inherit the session's effort. Choose the model per call with the Agent tool's `model` parameter. Use it for judgement work: a code review, a second opinion on a design, an audit of what a spec leaves unsaid, an executor of a multi-file stage that must decide as it goes. ([ilya-kozyrev/delamain](https://github.com/ilya-kozyrev/delamain/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-high.md) · ★5)
- **worker-high**: Escalation only: work an Opus medium worker failed through a reasoning problem (wrong cause, two failed fixes). Never a first assignment, never for landing, capacity, client or leash stops. ([tompassarelli/north](https://github.com/tompassarelli/north/blob/HEAD/harness/claude/agents/worker-high.md) · ★5)
- **worker-high**: Agent-team worker running at high effort. Takes any role (planner, researcher, builder, verifier, reviewer, tester) from a role file named in its prompt; the model is chosen per call. Used by the agent-team skill. ([111vandit111/ai-dev-team](https://github.com/111vandit111/ai-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-dev-team/agents/worker-high.md) · ★2)
- **worker-high**: Works one yakanban ticket end to end at high reasoning effort. Use for work that needs careful reasoning: concurrency, a design decision, a refactor across files. Pick the model per ticket when you launch it. ([aramponi/yakanban](https://github.com/aramponi/yakanban/blob/HEAD/site/demo/seed/.claude/agents/worker-high.md) · ★1)
- **worker-high**: Effort shim for /orchestrate — a sub-agent that inherits the parent's tools and runs on whatever model the dispatch names. Pass effort high on the Agent call itself; current Claude Code does not apply this file's effort frontmatter, so the type alone does not run at high. Not a role; carries no instructions of its own. ([davekim917/bootstrap](https://github.com/davekim917/bootstrap/blob/HEAD/plugins/orchestrate/agents/worker-high.md) · ★1)
- **worker-high**: High-effort routed worker for fable-router. Tricky debugging, cross-file refactors, retry after failed validation, and consequential independent second-opinion review. Combine with the Agent tool's model parameter to pick the model tier. ([KyongSik-Yoon/fable-router](https://github.com/KyongSik-Yoon/fable-router/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-high.md) · ★1)
- **worker-high**: Sonnet at high effort. Use ONLY for (1) complex reasoning (methodological/statistical/architectural ambiguity), (2) non-trivial code implementation, (3) difficult writing (manuscript sections, theory arguments), or (4) when the user explicitly asks for high/advanced reasoning. Everything else goes to the `worker` agent. ([suengj/ai-editorial-system](https://github.com/suengj/ai-editorial-system/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker-high.md) · ★1)
- **worker-high**: High-difficulty implementation worker for LARGE changes - new modules, cross-module refactors, concurrency/security/migration work, or complex problems with ONE clear goal. Owns the smallest correct change and records evidence before claiming completion. ([cameleonh/omo-adaptive](https://github.com/cameleonh/omo-adaptive/blob/HEAD/packages/omo-claude/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: General-purpose worker agent at high effort. Carries out the task the parent session gives it; the parent chooses the model per call. ([chelebyy/claude-kit](https://github.com/chelebyy/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: Implements tasks from todos - writes code, runs tests, commits with polished messages ([Dreagen/dotfiles](https://github.com/Dreagen/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.pi/agent/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: Implementation Worker for ordinary bounded implementation that needs some judgment (default choice for feature work and bug fixes). Owns one goal in its assigned checkout and does not delegate. Use worker for clear, mechanical work; complex-worker for multi-step or ambiguous work. ([esnssr/machine-dots](https://github.com/esnssr/machine-dots/blob/HEAD/portable/agents/claude/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: Escalation delegate for well-scoped execution steps that need deeper reasoning, such as concurrency, non-trivial algorithms, changes spanning many interacting modules, or a task a `worker` already attempted and got wrong. Not for root-causing bugs (debugger) or reviewing code for vulnerabilities (security-reviewer). Do not use for routine work. ([evictedcucumber/.claude](https://github.com/evictedcucumber/.claude/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: trimtab worker that runs one analysed part of a task at high effort. Use for parts the trimtab scout sized as high. ([ferhatblnk/trimtab](https://github.com/ferhatblnk/trimtab/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: The Worker at high effort. Use for the fix cycle after a review finding and for Production or Critical tiers; for everything else dispatch worker. ([galileo-studio-dev/skills](https://github.com/galileo-studio-dev/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: pstack internal: general-purpose worker at high effort. ([irfanalimd/pstack-ports](https://github.com/irfanalimd/pstack-ports/blob/HEAD/claude-code/pstack/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: Worker at high effort. The lead sets the model per call (sonnet, opus or fable). Default use: mechanical work on sonnet (extraction, tabulation, running written scripts, applying decided edits) and standard coding on opus. ([kirosli00tuff/PropExperiment](https://github.com/kirosli00tuff/PropExperiment/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: A worker for one hard or long task of a plan in docs/plans (for example a check rule and its specification, an interface design, or a large documentation rewrite). Sonnet at high effort. The main agent gives the task, the worktree and the plan sections, and reviews the diff. ([mdtisdall/pulseq-checks](https://github.com/mdtisdall/pulseq-checks/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker-high.md) · 2 copies)
- **worker-high**: A worker for one hard or long task of a plan in docs/plans (for example a JavaScript card script, an interface design, or a large documentation rewrite). Sonnet at high effort. The main agent gives the task, the worktree and the plan sections, and reviews the diff. ([mdtisdall/pulseq-reports](https://github.com/mdtisdall/pulseq-reports/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: High-tier implementation agent. Executes plan tasks marked `Execution tier: high` (non-UI) and fixes their review findings. ([smantzavinos/engineering-agents](https://github.com/smantzavinos/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-high**: General-purpose worker pinned to high reasoning effort. Research, code search, multi-step tasks. Pick the model via the Agent tool's model parameter. ([xylophoneengine/research-kit](https://github.com/xylophoneengine/research-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/research-kit/agents/worker-high.md))
- **worker-identity**: You are **{{name}}**, a **{{role}}** in the multi-agent orchestration system. ([adamancyzhang/claude-orchestrator-server](https://github.com/adamancyzhang/claude-orchestrator-server/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/worker-identity.md) · ★2)

- **worker-light**: Closed-spec implementation (Sonnet): executes an explicit spec — target files or area, the approach or a pattern to follow, and a checkable done condition. - **worker-light**: LIGHT tier (effort medium). Never use it for security, money, data or design-gated work. ([bartawilly/agent-lanes](https://github.com/bartawilly/agent-lanes/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-light.md) · ★1)
- **worker-light**: Лёгкий помощник общего назначения для CEO: найти, прочитать, разобрать, пересказать, отсортировать, простой скрипт по готовому рецепту. Не считает выводы и не решает; пишет только в scratchpad сессии, не в проект. Работа, где нужно думать (разбор данных, сверка, скрипт с логикой), — не ему, а тикет Инженеру или Исследователю. Smallest model. Not for long multi-step loops, very long prompts, judgement calls, facts from memory, or anything under confidential/. - **worker-light**: Sonnet 5.5 の軽量実装ワーカー。機械的な作業（改名、参照の更新、文書の書き換え、既存の形に倣った単純なテストの追加）を 1 件ずつ完遂する。設計判断やルール検査テストの新規作成は worker（Opus）に任せる。 ([d-kanai/ai-only-sample](https://github.com/d-kanai/ai-only-sample/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker-light.md))

- **worker-light**: Mechanical helper on the light tier (haiku). Executes one well-scoped work unit delegated by the orchestrator. Used by the /plan-and-delegate skill; see worker (complex) and worker-std (straightforward).
